# Inventory

Every file extracted from the 1.14d installer (`workspace/reference/installers/1.14d/`), with its hash.
The installer files themselves and the 1.14b reference set are hashed in `docs/formats/installer.md`. Generated by
`python3 -I tools/inventory.py workspace/install/app --class … ` (exact command under *Regenerate*).
Paths are relative to `workspace/install/app/`. Owned by `archivist`.

## PINNED

| file | size | sha256 |
|---|---:|---|
| `game.exe` | 3618792 | **`631066c1649c4ea9ffe48bf97e24c00bca1f7a6759c21150f1a79982589adaaf`** |

This is the project's identity (ADR-0001). Every `0x00XXXXXX` anchor refers to this file.
If a re-extraction produces a different hash, stop and raise it with the architect.

- `[C]` PE32 i386 GUI, image base `0x400000`, 5 sections (`.text .rdata .data .rsrc .reloc`),
  link timestamp `0x574DE0BC` = 2016-05-31 19:02:20 UTC, linker 8.0, entry RVA `0x282985`
  (`file(1)` and `tools/pe_info.py` agree on machine and section count).
- `[C]` Version resource: FileVersion **1.14.3.71**, ProductVersion string `1, 14, 3, 257 (79b7ae8)`,
  CompanyName Blizzard Entertainment, OriginalFilename `Diablo II.exe`. 1.14.3.71 is 1.14d.
- `[C]` **Unmodified Blizzard build.** `tools/pe_authenticode.py` (log: `workspace/logs/game-exe-authenticode.txt`):
  1. The Authenticode SHA-1 image digest `d15ed5aad055779776c30e8fcb2ed690876d7d80` is the one
     inside the signed SpcIndirectDataContent.
  2. The signer's messageDigest attribute equals SHA-1 of that content.
  3. The RSA signature over the signed attributes verifies with the signer certificate
     *Blizzard Entertainment, Inc.* (issuer DigiCert SHA2 Assured ID Code Signing CA, valid 2015-11-14..2018-01-18).
  4. The chain verifies to the system-trusted DigiCert Assured ID Root CA at the link timestamp.
  5. The stored PE CheckSum `0x37CED2` matches the recomputed one.
  Any byte change outside the certificate table would break step 1, so nothing in the install patched
  this binary (no no-CD crack and no loader stub on disk). The mods in the install hook it at run
  time instead (see `docs/formats/installer.md`).

Section hashes, for spotting any later copy that differs only partly:

| section | VA | raw size | sha256 |
|---|---|---:|---|
| `.text`  | `0x00401000` | `0x2CB000` | `b25b6db6c75540d7e030f3b2bffffbdc83fdb1290b00e1fa1875d74a2daab554` |
| `.rdata` | `0x006CC000` | `0x039000` | `8046468fd193062dd466043a24b29a2c11a24665dcc05b55b0dd7542721e7669` |
| `.data`  | `0x00705000` | `0x048000` | `789a965c0cb2b458167678911b0905e28bd7554b36c9fd72d32ddad1b79273bd` |
| `.rsrc`  | `0x00995000` | `0x002000` | `3f512bc7b853f77ae95f33ee56000b08da31eabf6194df5941981e4f4b38b9d2` |
| `.reloc` | `0x00997000` | `0x023000` | `7ec9d22329324075e746dfb2676282f74338053ee04f1f427c9e8589523986e2` |

## MPQ contents

Archive contents are not listed file by file here. The 12 reference archives (hashes, entry counts,
name recovery) and the merged gameplay tree (20,569 files, manifest with per-file sha256 in
`workspace/logs/merged-manifest.tsv`) are documented in [formats/mpq.md](formats/mpq.md). The excel
tables are catalogued in [tables/README.md](tables/README.md).

## Classes

| class | files | bytes | meaning |
|---|---:|---:|---|
| `game` | 4 | 4088640 | Blizzard (or Blizzard-redistributed) PE whose Authenticode signature verifies: `game.exe`, `ijl11.dll`, `smackw32.dll` (signer Blizzard), `binkw32.dll` (signer Take-Two/Rockstar, RAD Bink redistributable). |
| `game-unverified` | 13 | 1677874221 | Blizzard file names whose integrity we **cannot** prove: the 11 Blizzard MPQs (none carries a weak or strong MPQ signature, per `tools/mpq_verify.py`), `diablo ii.exe` (unsigned 2001 Blizzard North launcher stub, v1.0.0.1), `d2.lng` (unsigned 1997 resource-only DLL with Blizzard error strings, referenced by name in `game.exe`). |
| `third-party` | 27 | 290264775 | Bundled non-Blizzard files: PlugY 14.03, D2GL, SGD2FreeRes, `ddraw.dll`/`glide3x.dll` wrappers, language `.CMD` launchers, info text, icons, a 207 MiB Russian strategy-guide PDF. **Never merged into game data.** |
| `local-overrides` | 2070 | 1058336053 | Loose `data/local/` overrides: 2060 `sfx/*.wav`, 7 `video/*.bik`, and the `Use`/`Use_eng`/`Use_rus` language-index files. No excel or other gameplay data. |

## Regenerate

```bash
cd workspace/install && python3 -I ../../tools/inventory.py app \
  --class third-party 'plugy*' 'plugy/*' 'sgd2freeres*' 'd2gl*' 'ddraw.dll' 'glide3x.dll' '*.cmd' \
      'diablo_2_lod_info.txt' 'icon_i.ico' 'uninstall/*' '*.pdf' \
  --class local-overrides 'data/*' \
  --class game-unverified '*.mpq' 'diablo ii.exe' 'd2.lng'
```
